<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pledged her undying loyalty to the cause she had joined.  她宣誓对所投身的事业永葆忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ethereal</b></td><td>/iˈθɪriəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻巧精致的；非物质的，精神（上）的</td><td>an ethereal beauty<br>轻灵飘逸的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice had an ethereal quality that hushed the entire hall.  她的嗓音有种轻灵飘逸的质感，让全场安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluffy</td><td>/ˈflʌfi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻软的，蓬松的</td><td>fluffy white clouds<br>蓬松的白云</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitten was a fluffy ball of gray fur on my lap.  小猫是我膝上一团蓬松的灰毛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gossamer</td><td>/ˈɡɑːsəmər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>虚无缥缈的东西；轻薄的；薄弱的</td><td>gossamer wings<br>薄如蝉翼的翅膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her gossamer scarf floated on the breeze like a wisp of smoke.  她轻薄如蝉翼的围巾在微风中飘动，像一缕青烟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 light</td><td>/ˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻微的，不严重的</td><td>a light meal<br>一顿便餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffered only light injuries in the accident.  他在事故中只受了轻伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bodiless</td><td>/ˈbɑdiləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无形体的，非物质的</td><td>a bodiless voice<br>无形的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bodiless voice from the speakers announced the final boarding call.  扩音器里传出无形的声音宣布最后登机通知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 formless</td><td>/ˈfɔrmləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无形的，无固定形状的</td><td>a formless mass<br>无形的一团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fear was formless, attached to nothing he could actually name.  他的恐惧无形，没有依附于任何他能叫出名字的事物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incorporeal</td><td>/ˌɪnkɔrˈpɔriəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无形的，非肉体的</td><td>incorporeal spirits<br>无形的灵体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Medieval writers argued about whether angels were truly incorporeal beings.  中世纪作者争论天使是否真是无形的存在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insubstantial</td><td>/ˌɪnsəbˈstænʃəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非实在的；虚弱的</td><td>insubstantial evidence<br>站不住脚的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His case rested on insubstantial rumor rather than real documents.  他的论据靠的是虚无的传言而非真实文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nonmaterial</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnməˈtɪriəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非物质的，精神的</td><td>nonmaterial rewards<br>非物质的回报</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers work for nonmaterial rewards such as gratitude and shared purpose.  志愿者为感激与共同目标等非物质回报工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonphysical</td><td>/ˌnɑnˈfɪzɪkəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非物质的，非肉体的</td><td>nonphysical damage<br>非身体的损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawsuit claimed nonphysical harm such as anxiety and lost reputation.  诉讼主张焦虑与名誉损失等非身体伤害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spiritual</td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtʃuəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精神的；灵魂的，宗教的</td><td>spiritual growth<br>精神成长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She turned to meditation in search of spiritual peace.  她转向冥想，以寻求精神上的平静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heavy</td><td>/ˈhevi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重的，沉重的</td><td>too heavy to lift<br>重得抬不动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The box was so heavy that two men could barely move it.  这箱子太重，两个人几乎搬不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 leaden</td><td>/ˈledən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉重的，无生气的；铅灰的</td><td>a leaden sky<br>铅灰色的天空</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He walked home with leaden steps after the painful defeat.  痛苦的失败后，他步履沉重地走回家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ponderous</td><td>/ˈpɑːndərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉重的</td><td>ponderous machinery<br>笨重的机器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ponderous machinery filled the workshop and shook the floor all day.  笨重的机器占满车间，整天震动地板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bodily</td><td>/ˈbɑːdəli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>身体的，肉体的</td><td>bodily harm<br>身体伤害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The law protects citizens from any threat of bodily harm.  法律保护公民免受任何身体伤害的威胁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 corporeal</td><td>/kɔːrˈpɔːriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的；有形的，实体的</td><td>corporeal existence<br>肉体的存在</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ghost seemed almost corporeal in the flickering candlelight.  在摇曳的烛光中，那鬼影几乎像有实体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 material</td><td>/məˈtɪriəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>物质的；重要的；材料</td><td>material comforts<br>物质上的享受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cared little for material wealth and lived very simply.  他不太在意物质财富，生活极为简朴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 physical</td><td>/ˈfɪzɪkəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>身体的；物质的</td><td>physical evidence<br>实物证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police found no physical evidence linking him to the crime.  警方没有找到把他与罪案联系起来的实物证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 substantial</td><td>/səbˈstænʃl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，可观的；实质的</td><td>a substantial increase<br>大幅的增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reported a substantial increase in profits this quarter.  公司报告本季度利润大幅增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ethics</b></td><td>/ˈeθɪks/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>道德规范</td><td>a code of ethics<br>道德准则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee revised its code of ethics after the scandal.  丑闻之后委员会修订了道德规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ethos</td><td>/ˈiːθɑːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>道德准则</td><td>the company ethos<br>公司的精神准则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teamwork lies at the heart of this small company's ethos.  团队合作是这家小公司精神准则的核心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morality</td><td>/məˈræləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>道德；德行</td><td>public morality<br>公共道德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal raised hard questions about morality in politics.  这桩丑闻引出了政治道德的难题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morals</td><td>/ˈmɔːrəlz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>道德，品行</td><td>loose morals<br>品行不端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel questions whether wartime morals differ from those of peacetime.  小说质问战时道德是否与和平时期不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 norms</td><td>/ˈnɔːrmz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>规范，准则</td><td>social norms<br>社会规范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Newcomers quickly learn the unwritten norms of that tight-knit workshop.  新来者很快学会那间紧密作坊的不成文规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 principles</td><td>/ˈprɪnsəpəlz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>原则，准则</td><td>stick to his principles<br>坚守原则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She resigned rather than betray the principles she had taught for years.  她宁愿辞职也不背弃多年所教的原则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 standards</td><td>/ˈstændərdz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>标准，准则</td><td>high ethical standards<br>高道德标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic holds all its staff to strict hygiene standards.  诊所要求全体员工遵守严格的卫生标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>eulogize</b></td><td>/ˈjuːlədʒaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>称赞；颂扬</td><td>eulogize the late leader<br>颂扬已故领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Colleagues gathered to eulogize the scientist who had trained them all.  同事们聚在一起颂扬这位培养了他们所有人的科学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defame</td><td>/dɪˈfeɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，中伤</td><td>defame a public figure<br>诽谤公众人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sued the paper for trying to defame him.  他起诉那家报纸企图诽谤他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pan</td><td>/pæn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>严厉批评</td><td>pan a new film<br>严厉批评一部新片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics panned the film for its clumsy dialogue and predictable ending.  影评人严厉批评这部电影对话笨拙、结局可预料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>euphemism</b></td><td>/ˈjuːfəmɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>婉言，委婉的说法</td><td>a polite euphemism<br>礼貌的委婉说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Downsizing is often just a euphemism for firing people.  精简规模常常只是解雇员工的委婉说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>euphonious</b></td><td>/juːˈfoʊniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悦耳的</td><td>a euphonious name<br>悦耳的名字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet chose euphonious words that pleased the ear when read aloud.  诗人选用朗读时悦耳的词语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mellifluous</td><td>/meˈlɪfluəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（声音）甜美流畅的</td><td>a mellifluous voice<br>甜美悦耳的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The narrator's mellifluous voice made even the dullest chapters pleasant to listen to.  叙述者优美悦耳的嗓音让最枯燥的章节也变得动听。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 melodious</td><td>/məˈloʊdiəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悦耳的，旋律优美的</td><td>a melodious voice<br>悦耳的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her melodious laugh carried across the courtyard on the still evening.  她悦耳的笑声在静夜里穿过庭院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 canorous</del></td><td>/kəˈnɔrəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>响亮悦耳的</td><td>a canorous chant<br>洪亮悦耳的吟唱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks' canorous chanting filled the stone hall every morning.  僧人洪亮悦耳的吟唱每天早晨充满石厅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harmonizing</td><td>/ˈhɑːrməˌnaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐的，协调一致的</td><td>harmonizing colors<br>协调的色彩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two harmonizing melodies rose together at the end of the song.  两段和谐的旋律在歌曲结尾一同升起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 symphonious</del></td><td>/sɪmˈfoʊniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐悦耳的</td><td>symphonious birdsong<br>和谐悦耳的鸟鸣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dawn brought a symphonious mixture of birdsong to the quiet valley.  黎明给静谷带来和谐悦耳的鸟鸣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tuneful</td><td>/ˈtuːnfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悦耳的，曲调优美的</td><td>a tuneful melody<br>优美的旋律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The musical is remembered for its tuneful songs.  这部音乐剧因旋律优美的歌曲而被记住。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cacophonous</td><td>/kəˈkɑfənəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的，不和谐的</td><td>a cacophonous racket<br>刺耳的喧闹声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The orchestra produced a cacophonous din while tuning up.  乐队调音时发出刺耳的嘈杂声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discordant</td><td>/dɪˈskɔːrdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，不和谐的</td><td>discordant opinions<br>不一致的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Discordant voices from the back ruined the quiet ceremony.  后面不和谐的声音破坏了安静的仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disharmonious</del></td><td>/ˌdɪshɑrˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不和谐的，不一致的</td><td>a disharmonious relationship<br>不和睦的关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their disharmonious partnership collapsed within a single season.  他们不和的合作关系在一个赛季内就崩溃了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissonant</td><td>/ˈdɪsənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不和谐的，不一致的</td><td>dissonant voices<br>不和谐的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dissonant chord broke the calm mood of the piece.  一个不和谐的和弦打破了乐曲平静的气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tuneless</td><td>/ˈtuːnləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不成调的，不悦耳的</td><td>tuneless humming<br>不成调的哼唱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He whistled a tuneless melody while waiting for the bus.  他等公交时吹着不成调的口哨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unmelodious</del></td><td>/ˌʌnməˈloʊdiəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不悦耳的，不成旋律的</td><td>an unmelodious screech<br>刺耳难听的尖叫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old violin produced an unmelodious sound that emptied the room.  那把旧小提琴发出难听的声音，把屋里人都赶走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>euphoria</b></td><td>/juːˈfɔːriə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>极度愉快，狂喜</td><td>a mood of national euphoria<br>全国上下的狂喜情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The euphoria after the victory faded within a couple of days.  胜利后的极度愉快在几天之内就消退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elation</td><td>/ɪˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>得意，兴高采烈</td><td>a feeling of elation<br>兴高采烈之感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her elation faded once she read the fine print carefully.  她仔细读了小字条款后，兴奋就消退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhilaration</td><td>/ɪɡˌzɪləˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>兴奋，极度愉快</td><td>a sense of exhilaration<br>一种兴奋感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt pure exhilaration as the glider rose above the valley.  滑翔机升到山谷上方时，她感到无比兴奋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intoxication</td><td>/ˌɪntɑːksəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极度兴奋，陶醉；醉酒</td><td>the intoxication of success<br>成功带来的陶醉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The intoxication of victory faded once the hard rebuilding work began.  艰难重建开始后，胜利的陶醉便消退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transport</td><td>/trænsˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>运输，运送</td><td>transport the goods<br>运送货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trucks transport fresh produce to the city every morning.  卡车每天早晨把新鲜农产品运往城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rapture</td><td>/ˈræptʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>极度欢喜，狂喜</td><td>listen in rapture<br>如痴如醉地聆听</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audience listened in rapture as the old singer began her final song.  老歌手唱起最后一曲时，观众听得如痴如醉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rhapsody</td><td>/ˈræpsədi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂喜，热情赞美；狂想曲</td><td>go into rhapsodies<br>大加赞美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He went into rhapsodies about the noodles he ate in Osaka.  他对在大阪吃的面条大加赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depression</td><td>/dɪˈpreʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>沮丧，抑郁；萧条</td><td>sink into deep depression<br>陷入深度抑郁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing his job he slid into a long depression.  失业之后，他陷入了长久的抑郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>evacuate</b></td><td>/ɪˈvækjueɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤离</td><td>evacuate the building<br>撤离大楼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters evacuated the entire building before the roof collapsed.  消防员在屋顶塌落前撤离了整栋大楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vacate</td><td>/ˈveɪkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>腾空，搬出；辞去</td><td>vacate the premises<br>腾出房屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tenants must vacate the apartment before noon on the last day.  租户必须在最后一天中午前搬出公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 void</td><td>/ˈvɔɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词、名词</td><td>使无效；排空；空虚</td><td>void the contract<br>使合同无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missing a single payment can void the entire warranty agreement.  漏付一期就可能使整个保修协议失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fill up</td><td>/fɪl ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>装满，填满</td><td>fill up the tank<br>把油箱加满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We filled up the tank before crossing the long desert road.  我们在穿越漫长沙漠公路前把油箱加满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 occupy</td><td>/ˈɑːkjəˌpaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>占据，占用；占领</td><td>occupy the whole floor<br>占据整层楼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops occupied the town for six months before withdrawing quietly.  部队占领该镇六个月后悄然撤离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 load</td><td>/ˈloʊd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>装载，装满；负荷</td><td>load the truck<br>装货上卡车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers loaded the truck with sacks of rice before dawn.  工人们在黎明前把一袋袋大米装上卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>evanescent</b></td><td>/ˌevəˈnesnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，逐渐消失的</td><td>evanescent beauty<br>转瞬即逝的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evanescent glow of fireflies lit the summer path.  萤火虫短暂的微光照亮了夏夜小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fleeting</td><td>/ˈfliːtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；转瞬即逝的</td><td>a fleeting moment<br>短暂的瞬间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For one fleeting moment the clouds parted above the valley.  有一个短暂的瞬间，云在山谷上方散开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impermanent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非永久的，短暂的</td><td>impermanent beauty<br>短暂的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All fashions are impermanent, replaced by new styles within a few seasons.  一切时尚都是短暂的，几季之内就被新风格取代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temporary</td><td>/ˈtempəreri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暂时的，临时的</td><td>a temporary solution<br>临时的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company hired temporary staff to handle the busy holiday season.  公司雇了临时员工来应对繁忙的假日季。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transient</td><td>/ˈtrænʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；暂时的；过往的</td><td>transient population<br>流动人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fame in the internet age is often transient and quickly forgotten.  网络时代的名声往往短暂，很快被人遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transitory</td><td>/ˈtrænsətɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的</td><td>transitory pleasure<br>短暂的快乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was transitory and he apologized within the hour.  他的愤怒很短暂，一小时内就道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ceaseless</td><td>/ˈsiːslɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>ceaseless noise<br>不停的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceaseless traffic outside made it impossible for us to sleep.  外面不停的车流让我们无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 endless</td><td>/ˈendləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，无休止的</td><td>endless complaints<br>无休止的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meeting felt endless, though it lasted only ninety minutes.  会议感觉没完没了，其实只开了九十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enduring</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，长存的</td><td>an enduring friendship<br>长久的友谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their enduring friendship survived decades of separation.  他们持久的友谊经历了数十年的分离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 everlasting</td><td>/ˌevərˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，持久的</td><td>everlasting peace<br>永久的和平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They vowed everlasting loyalty to each other.  他们发誓永远彼此忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immortal</td><td>/ɪˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不朽的，永生的</td><td>immortal fame<br>不朽的声名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet's immortal lines are still quoted two thousand years later.  这位诗人不朽的诗句两千年后仍被引用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 permanent</td><td>/ˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>a permanent solution<br>永久的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They are seeking a permanent solution rather than another quick fix.  他们在寻找永久的解决办法，而不是又一个权宜之计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 timeless</td><td>/ˈtaɪmləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不受时间影响的</td><td>timeless design<br>经典不过时的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dress has a timeless elegance that never dates.  这条裙子有一种永不过时的优雅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undying</td><td>/ʌnˈdaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不灭的</td><td>undying love<br>永恒的爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pledged her undying loyalty to the cause she had joined.  她宣誓对所投身的事业永葆忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ending</td><td>/ˈendɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结局，结尾</td><td>a happy ending<br>幸福的结局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel has an ending that nobody sees coming.  这部小说的结局谁都想不到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abiding</td><td>/əˈbaɪdɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，长久的</td><td>an abiding interest<br>持久的兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has an abiding love for classical music.  她对古典音乐有着持久的热爱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lasting</td><td>/ˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，永久的</td><td>a lasting impression<br>持久的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her kindness left a lasting impression on everyone in the ward.  她的善意给病房里每个人留下持久印象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>entail</b></td><td>/ɪnˈteɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>牵涉，导致</td><td>entail considerable risk<br>牵涉相当大的风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rebuilding the bridge would entail considerable risk to the workers below.  重建这座桥会给下方的工人带来相当大的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contain</td><td>/kənˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>控制，抑制；包含</td><td>contain the outbreak<br>控制疫情爆发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors worked around the clock to contain the outbreak.  医生们昼夜不停地努力控制疫情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 embrace</td><td>/ɪmˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>乐于接受；拥抱</td><td>embrace change<br>乐于接受变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young workers quickly embraced the new technology.  年轻员工迅速接受了这项新技术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encompass</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmpəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>包含，涵盖；包围</td><td>encompass every major topic<br>涵盖所有主要话题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single volume can hardly encompass every major topic in modern physics.  一卷书很难涵盖现代物理学的所有主要话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 include</td><td>/ɪnˈkluːd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>包含，包括</td><td>include all the costs<br>包含全部费用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The price includes all the costs of shipping and insurance.  价格包含全部运费和保险费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involve</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>涉及，包含；使参与</td><td>involve considerable extra work<br>涉及大量额外工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan involves moving three departments to another building.  这个计划涉及把三个部门迁到另一栋楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exclude</td><td>/ɪkˈskluːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>排除，不包括</td><td>exclude him from the list<br>把他从名单中排除</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The price excludes taxes and delivery to remote areas.  该价格不包括税费和偏远地区的运费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 miss out</td><td>/mɪs ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>错过，遗漏</td><td>miss out on chances<br>错过机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Arrive early so you do not miss out on the free samples.  早点到，别错过免费样品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 omit</td><td>/oʊˈmɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>省略，遗漏，忽略</td><td>omit the details<br>省略细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The summary omits the details that made his argument convincing.  摘要省略了使他论证有说服力的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her ageless beauty surprised everyone at the reunion.  她不显岁月的美貌让聚会上的人都惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enduring</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，长存的</td><td>an enduring friendship<br>长久的友谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their enduring friendship survived decades of separation.  他们持久的友谊经历了数十年的分离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immortal</td><td>/ɪˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不朽的，永生的</td><td>immortal fame<br>不朽的声名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet's immortal lines are still quoted two thousand years later.  这位诗人不朽的诗句两千年后仍被引用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperishable</td><td>/ɪmˈperɪʃəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不朽的，永恒的</td><td>imperishable glory<br>不朽的荣耀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldiers won imperishable glory defending the bridge for three days.  士兵们守桥三天赢得了不朽的荣耀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perennial</td><td>/pəˈreniəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>长期的，反复出现的</td><td>a perennial problem<br>长期存在的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic is a perennial problem in this old city.  交通是这座老城长期存在的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undying</td><td>/ʌnˈdaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不灭的</td><td>undying love<br>永恒的爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pledged her undying loyalty to the cause she had joined.  她宣誓对所投身的事业永葆忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impermanent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非永久的，短暂的</td><td>impermanent beauty<br>短暂的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All fashions are impermanent, replaced by new styles within a few seasons.  一切时尚都是短暂的，几季之内就被新风格取代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mortal</td><td>/ˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>终将死亡的，凡人的；致命的</td><td>mortal beings<br>凡人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play reminds us that even great kings are mortal men.  这部剧提醒我们连伟大的国王也是凡人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 temporary</td><td>/ˈtempəreri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暂时的，临时的</td><td>a temporary solution<br>临时的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company hired temporary staff to handle the busy holiday season.  公司雇了临时员工来应对繁忙的假日季。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 transient</td><td>/ˈtrænʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；暂时的；过往的</td><td>transient population<br>流动人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fame in the internet age is often transient and quickly forgotten.  网络时代的名声往往短暂，很快被人遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>evict</b></td><td>/ɪˈvɪkt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赶出，逐出</td><td>evict the tenants<br>驱逐房客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The landlord threatened to evict the tenants who had not paid.  房东威胁要赶走那些没交房租的房客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harbor</td><td>/ˈhɑːrbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>藏匿，庇护；心怀（怨恨）</td><td>harbor a deep grudge<br>心怀深深怨恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family was accused of harboring a fugitive for several months.  这家人被指控藏匿一名逃犯数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>evince</b></td><td>/ɪˈvɪns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>表明</td><td>evince little interest<br>表现出兴趣不大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flat answers evinced very little interest in the job.  他平淡的回答表明他对这份工作兴趣不大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bespeak</td><td>/bɪˈspiːk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>表明，显示；预示</td><td>bespeak great care<br>显示出极大用心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neat handwriting bespeaks a patience few students still possess.  整洁的字迹表明少有学生仍具备的耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 betray</td><td>/bɪˈtreɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>无意中显露，表明；背叛</td><td>betray his nervousness<br>显露他的紧张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His trembling hands betrayed the fear his calm voice concealed.  他颤抖的双手泄露了平静语调所掩藏的恐惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 declare</td><td>/dɪˈkler/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>宣布，声明，断言</td><td>declare one's intentions<br>表明自己的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge declared the earlier verdict invalid and ordered a retrial.  法官宣布先前判决无效并下令重审。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demonstrate</td><td>/ˈdemənstreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过证据）证明，表明</td><td>demonstrate the theory clearly<br>清楚地证明该理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The experiment demonstrated the theory clearly to every skeptical observer.  实验向每位持疑的观察者清楚证明了该理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expose</td><td>/ɪkˈspoʊz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露；使暴露</td><td>expose the fraud<br>揭露欺诈行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two journalists exposed the fraud after months of quiet investigation.  两名记者经过数月暗中调查揭露了这起欺诈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manifest</td><td>/ˈmænɪfest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>显然的，明显易懂的；显现，显露</td><td>manifest injustice<br>明显的不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discomfort at the question was manifest to everyone seated around the table.  他对这个问题的不适让围坐桌边的每个人都看得明明白白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reveal</td><td>/rɪˈviːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭示，透露</td><td>reveal the details<br>透露细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company refused to reveal the details of the settlement.  公司拒绝透露和解的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 give away</td><td>/ɡɪv əˈweɪ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>泄露，暴露；赠送</td><td>give away the secret<br>泄露秘密</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark gave away the surprise we had planned for months.  一句不慎的话泄露了我们筹划数月的惊喜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conceal</td><td>/kənˈsiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>conceal the truth<br>隐瞒真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness tried to conceal the truth from the police.  证人试图向警方隐瞒真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 keep hidden</td><td>/kiːp ˈhɪdən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>keep hidden from view<br>藏起不让看见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family kept the letters hidden in a drawer for decades.  这家人把信藏在抽屉里几十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>evoke</b></td><td>/ɪˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>唤起，引发</td><td>evoke childhood memories<br>唤起童年回忆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of wet chalk evokes my primary school classroom.  湿粉笔的味道让我想起小学的教室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elicit</td><td>/iˈlɪsɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，唤起</td><td>elicit a strong reaction<br>引起强烈反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king's speech elicited sustained cheers and applause from the crowd.  国王的演讲激起了人群持久的欢呼和掌声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inspire</td><td>/ɪnˈspaɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓舞，激励；引发</td><td>inspire confidence in others<br>让他人产生信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech inspired the students to work much harder.  她的演讲激励学生更加努力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raise</td><td>/ˈreɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提出（问题）；举起，提高</td><td>raise an objection<br>提出反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He raised an objection just before the vote was taken.  就在投票前他提出了反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exacerbate</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzæsərbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧，使恶化</td><td>exacerbate the crisis<br>使危机加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the budget would only exacerbate the housing shortage.  削减预算只会使住房短缺进一步恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complicate</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复杂化</td><td>complicate matters further<br>使事情更复杂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence complicated the investigation and delayed the final report.  新证据使调查复杂化，并推迟了最终报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exacting</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzæktɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，苛求的；费时间花心思的</td><td>exacting quality standards<br>严格的质量标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline maintains exacting standards for every part it installs.  这家航空公司对安装的每个部件都保持严格的标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 choosy</td><td>/ˈtʃuːzi/</td><td>★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，苛求的</td><td>choosy about food<br>对食物挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is so choosy about coffee that he grinds his own beans.  他对咖啡挑剔到自己磨豆子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demanding</td><td>/dɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难取悦的，难满足的；费时间花心思的</td><td>a demanding new job<br>费心费力的新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for three small children is demanding work with no holidays.  照顾三个小孩是费时间花心思且没有休假的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fastidious</td><td>/fæˈstɪdiəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，极仔细的，追求完美的</td><td>fastidious about details<br>对细节极挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is fastidious about details and rereads every line twice.  她对细节极为挑剔，每一行都要读两遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 finical</td><td>/ˈfɪnɪkəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过分挑剔的</td><td>a finical eater<br>挑食的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their finical guest rejected almost every dish on the table.  他们挑剔的客人几乎拒绝了桌上每道菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fussy</td><td>/ˈfʌsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，过分讲究的</td><td>fussy about small details<br>对小细节很挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old tailor is fussy about the smallest details.  这位老裁缝对最小的细节都很挑剔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pernickety</del></td><td>/pərˈnɪkəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过分讲究的，挑剔的</td><td>pernickety about details<br>对细节过分挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our editor is pernickety about commas but forgiving about long sentences.  我们的编辑对逗号极挑剔，对长句却宽容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 persnickety</td><td>/pərˈsnɪkəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过分挑剔的</td><td>a persnickety inspector<br>过分挑剔的检查员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The persnickety inspector rejected the wall over one small crack.  这位挑剔的检查员因一条小裂缝就否决了那面墙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 picky</td><td>/ˈpɪki/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，难满足的</td><td>a picky eater<br>挑食的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My nephew is a picky eater who refuses all vegetables.  我侄子很挑食，什么蔬菜都不吃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arduous</td><td>/ˈɑːrdʒuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以做到的，费劲的</td><td>an arduous journey<br>艰苦的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climb proved far more arduous than any of them expected.  这次攀登远比他们任何人预想的都费劲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burdensome</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdənsəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁重的，累人的</td><td>burdensome regulations<br>繁琐的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new paperwork proved burdensome for small rural clinics.  新的文书工作对乡村小诊所来说很繁重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 challenging</td><td>/ˈtʃæləndʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有挑战性的，费心力的</td><td>a challenging assignment<br>有挑战性的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor gave us a challenging assignment due next week.  教授给了我们一个下周交的有挑战性作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grueling</td><td>/ˈɡruːəlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>累人的，折磨人的</td><td>a grueling schedule<br>折磨人的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team endured a grueling schedule of twelve games in three weeks.  球队熬过了三周十二场比赛这种折磨人的赛程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 killing</td><td>/ˈkɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>极累人的，要命的</td><td>a killing work schedule<br>要命的工作日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They kept a killing schedule of twelve shows every week.  他们保持着每周十二场演出的要命日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laborious</td><td>/ləˈbɔːriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰苦的；勤奋的</td><td>a laborious process<br>费力的过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying the manuscript by hand proved a slow and laborious process.  手抄这份手稿是一个缓慢而费力的过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 onerous</td><td>/ˈɑːnərəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁重的，费力的</td><td>onerous responsibilities<br>繁重的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for the archive proved more onerous than the volunteers had expected.  照管这份档案比志愿者们预想的更费时费心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taxing</td><td>/ˈtæksɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁重的，费力的</td><td>a taxing task<br>繁重的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for two infants proved a taxing task for the young couple.  照顾两个婴儿对这对年轻夫妻是繁重的任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 toilsome</del></td><td>/ˈtɔɪlsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辛苦的，费力的</td><td>toilsome labor<br>辛苦的劳作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying manuscripts by hand was toilsome work that ruined many eyes.  手抄书稿是毁坏许多人眼睛的辛苦活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 condone</td><td>/kənˈdoʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宽恕；忽视</td><td>condone the violence<br>宽恕这种暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school will never condone violence between its students.  学校绝不会宽恕学生之间的暴力行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forgive</td><td>/fərˈɡɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宽容，原谅</td><td>forgive a small mistake<br>原谅小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She forgave him quickly, though the insult had stung for days.  她很快原谅了他，尽管那侮辱刺痛了她几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 light</td><td>/ˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻微的，不严重的</td><td>a light meal<br>一顿便餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffered only light injuries in the accident.  他在事故中只受了轻伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unchallenging</td><td>/ˌʌnˈtʃælɪndʒɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有挑战性的，轻松的</td><td>an unchallenging job<br>没有挑战的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He quit the unchallenging position after only three months.  他只干了三个月就辞掉那份没有挑战性的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undemanding</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>要求不高的，轻松的</td><td>an undemanding course<br>要求不高的课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She chose an undemanding role so she could rest more.  她选了个轻松的角色以便多休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exalt</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬；提升（地位）</td><td>exalt to the skies<br>推崇到极点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics exalt her early novels as masterpieces of the era.  评论家将她早期的小说赞为那个时代的杰作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 emblazon</del></td><td>/emˈbleɪzən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，颂扬；用纹章装饰</td><td>emblazon his name<br>颂扬他的名字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old chronicle emblazons the deeds of the founding kings.  古老的编年史颂扬开国君王的功业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 condemn</td><td>/kənˈdem/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责（…为不道德的、邪恶的）</td><td>condemn the attack<br>谴责这次袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 World leaders quickly condemned the attack on the hospital.  各国领导人迅速谴责了对医院的袭击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exasperate</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzæspəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，激怒</td><td>exasperate his parents<br>使他的父母恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His constant lateness exasperated even the most patient teachers.  他总是迟到，连最有耐心的老师都被激怒了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gall</td><td>/ɡɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（使）焦躁，激怒</td><td>galled by the remark<br>被那句话惹恼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His smug tone galled everyone sitting around the conference table.  他自鸣得意的语气激怒了围坐在会议桌旁的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nettle</td><td>/ˈnetl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>惹怒</td><td>nettled by criticism<br>被批评惹怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was clearly nettled by the reporter's rude question.  记者无礼的提问显然惹恼了她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peeve</td><td>/piːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打扰，惹恼</td><td>peeve the audience<br>惹恼观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud phone conversations on the train really peeve the other passengers.  火车上大声打电话确实惹恼其他乘客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rile</td><td>/raɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，惹怒</td><td>rile up the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing riles him more than being interrupted in the middle of a sentence.  没有什么比话说到一半被打断更让他恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruffle</td><td>/ˈrʌfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>弄乱（毛发）；扰乱，使恼怒</td><td>ruffle his feathers<br>惹他不快</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single rude question was enough to ruffle the normally calm witness.  一个无礼的问题就足以扰乱这位平素冷静的证人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vex</td><td>/veks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使恼怒</td><td>vex the teacher<br>使老师恼怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant interruptions vexed the teacher throughout the long afternoon lesson.  不断的打扰让老师在漫长的下午课上十分恼怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burn up</td><td>/bɜrn ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使愤怒；烧尽</td><td>burn up with anger<br>怒火中烧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That careless remark really burned me up for the rest of the day.  那句不经心的话让我一整天都很生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>excavate</b></td><td>/ˈekskəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>挖掘，挖空</td><td>excavate an ancient tomb<br>挖掘古墓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists excavated the ancient tomb layer by careful layer.  考古学家一层一层小心地挖掘那座古墓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fill in</td><td>/fɪl ˈɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>填满，填补；填写</td><td>fill in the hole<br>把洞填上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers filled in the trench as soon as the pipes were laid.  管道铺好后工人立刻把沟填上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exceptional</b></td><td>/ɪkˈsepʃənl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>例外的，特别的，非凡的</td><td>an exceptional talent<br>非凡的天赋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed exceptional talent for mathematics at a very young age.  她在很小的年纪就表现出非凡的数学天赋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aberrant</td><td>/æˈberənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>异常的，非常规的</td><td>aberrant behavior<br>异常行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors were puzzled by the patient's aberrant test results.  医生对病人异常的化验结果感到困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abnormal</td><td>/æbˈnɔːrməl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>异常的，不正常的</td><td>abnormal test results<br>异常的检测结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scan showed nothing abnormal in her lungs.  扫描显示她的肺部没有异常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anomalous</td><td>/əˈnɑːmələs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不平常的，异常的</td><td>anomalous readings<br>异常的读数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team discarded a few anomalous readings from the data.  团队剔除了数据中几个异常读数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exceeding</td><td>/ɪkˈsiːdɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极度的，非常的</td><td>exceeding kindness<br>极大的善意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He showed exceeding patience with students who kept asking the same question.  他对反复问同一问题的学生表现出极大耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extraordinary</td><td>/ɪkˈstrɔːrdəneri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非凡的，特别的</td><td>extraordinary courage<br>非凡的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took extraordinary courage to report what she had seen.  举报她所见之事需要非凡的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peculiar</td><td>/pəˈkjuːliər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奇怪的；特有的</td><td>a peculiar smell<br>一股怪味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a peculiar smell coming from the basement.  地下室传来一股奇怪的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unwonted</del></td><td>/ʌnˈwoʊntɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不寻常的，不习惯的</td><td>unwonted silence<br>异乎寻常的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unwonted silence fell over the noisy classroom.  嘈杂的教室里出现了不寻常的安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commonplace</td><td>/ˈkɑːmənpleɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>平凡的；老生常谈</td><td>a commonplace observation<br>老生常谈的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Video calls have become commonplace in ordinary office life.  视频通话在日常办公中已很平常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prosaic</td><td>/prəˈzeɪɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，常见的</td><td>prosaic details<br>平淡的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Behind the legend lies a prosaic story about a lost receipt.  传说背后是一个关于遗失收据的平淡故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 customary</td><td>/ˈkʌstəˌmeri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>习惯的，惯常的</td><td>the customary greeting<br>惯常的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is customary here to remove your shoes at the door.  这里习惯在门口脱鞋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 normal</td><td>/ˈnɔːrməl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>正常的；常态</td><td>back to normal<br>恢复正常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everything returned to normal after the power was restored.  供电恢复后一切都回归正常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ordinary</td><td>/ˈɔːrdəneri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普通的，平常的</td><td>just an ordinary weekday<br>只是平常的工作日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He looked like an ordinary commuter waiting for the morning bus.  他看上去就像个等早班公交的普通上班族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 typical</td><td>/ˈtɪpəkəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>典型的，一贯的</td><td>a typical example<br>典型的例子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This building is a typical example of the local style.  这栋建筑是当地风格的典型代表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unextraordinary</del></td><td>/ˌʌnɪkˈstrɔːrdəneri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平常的，不非凡的</td><td>an unextraordinary life<br>平常的一生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led a quiet, unextraordinary life in a small town.  他在一个小镇过着平静普通的一生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 usual</td><td>/ˈjuːʒuəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>通常的，惯常的</td><td>the usual routine<br>惯常的程序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived at his usual time and took his usual seat.  他按老时间到达，坐在老位子上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>excoriate</b></td><td>/ɪkˈskɔːrieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；擦伤皮肤</td><td>excoriate the government<br>严厉批评政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics excoriated the film for its casual cruelty.  批评家严厉批评该片的随意残忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abuse</td><td>/əˈbjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辱骂，抨击；不正当或不合理使用</td><td>abuse of power<br>滥用权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of abusing his position for profit.  他被指控滥用职位谋利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assail</td><td>/əˈseɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，严厉批评</td><td>assail a policy<br>抨击某项政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opponents assailed the plan as reckless and unfair.  反对者抨击该计划轻率且不公。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belabor</td><td>/bɪˈleɪbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>过分强调；痛打</td><td>belabor the point<br>反复纠缠一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is no need to belabor the point any further.  没有必要再对这一点过分强调下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 castigate</td><td>/ˈkæstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈（公开）指责</td><td>castigate for negligence<br>因疏忽而受斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge castigated the company for its negligence.  法官严厉指责该公司的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lambaste</td><td>/læmˈbeɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉斥责，痛骂</td><td>lambaste the government<br>痛斥政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lambasted the film for its shallow, sentimental ending.  批评家严厉斥责这部电影结局浅薄而煽情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 vituperate</del></td><td>/vaɪˈtuːpəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谩骂，责骂</td><td>vituperate an opponent<br>谩骂对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist vituperated the mayor in every weekly article.  这位专栏作家在每周的文章里都痛骂市长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accolade</td><td>/ˈækəleɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>赞扬，荣誉；奖励</td><td>win the highest accolade<br>赢得最高荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received accolades from critics on both continents.  这部小说获得了两大洲评论家的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flatter</td><td>/ˈflætər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>奉承；讨好；谄媚</td><td>flatter the boss<br>奉承老板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He flattered the boss all morning hoping for a promotion.  他整个上午奉承老板，希望得到升职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise lavishly</td><td>/preɪz ˈlævɪʃli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>大加赞扬</td><td>praise lavishly in public<br>公开大加赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics praised the film lavishly, calling it the year's finest work.  评论家对这部电影大加赞扬，称其为年度最佳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>excrete</b></td><td>/ɪkˈskriːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>排泄</td><td>excrete waste products<br>排出废物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Kidneys excrete waste that would otherwise poison the bloodstream.  肾脏排出否则会毒害血液的废物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absorb</td><td>/əbˈzɔːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使全神贯注；吸收</td><td>be absorbed in the book<br>沉浸在书里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puzzle absorbed him completely for the whole afternoon.  这道谜题让他整个下午都全神贯注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ingest</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摄入，咽下</td><td>ingest contaminated water<br>摄入受污染的水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers who ingest contaminated water often develop serious stomach illnesses.  摄入受污染水源的村民常患严重的胃病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>excruciate</b></del></td><td>/ɪkˈskruːʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦</td><td>excruciate the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The interrogators excruciated the prisoners for information they never had.  审讯者折磨囚犯，逼问他们从未掌握的情报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agonize</td><td>/ˈæɡənaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）非常痛苦，苦苦思索</td><td>agonize over a decision<br>为决定苦苦纠结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She agonized for weeks over whether to accept the job.  她为是否接受这份工作苦恼了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anguish</td><td>/ˈæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>极度痛苦，苦恼</td><td>in great anguish<br>极度痛苦之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face showed the anguish of a sleepless night.  他的脸上写着一夜无眠的痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plague</td><td>/pleɪɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>瘟疫；折磨，使…痛苦</td><td>plagued by doubts<br>被疑虑折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doubts about the decision plagued him for the rest of the year.  对那个决定的疑虑折磨了他余下的一整年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rack</td><td>/ˈræk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；架子</td><td>racked with pain<br>受痛苦折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was racked by guilt long after the incident was forgotten.  事件被淡忘后他仍长久受负罪感折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torment</td><td>/tɔːrˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；痛苦</td><td>torment the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guilt tormented him for years after the accident.  事故之后，负罪感折磨了他多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harrow</td><td>/ˈhæroʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使苦恼</td><td>harrow his conscience<br>折磨他的良心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The memory of that night harrowed him for the rest of his life.  那一夜的记忆折磨了他的余生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exult</td><td>/ɪɡˈzʌlt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>感到欢喜</td><td>exult in her victory<br>为她的胜利而欢喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fans exulted in the victory long after the final whistle.  终场哨响很久之后球迷们仍在为胜利欢喜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exculpate</b></td><td>/ˈekskʌlpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声明无罪；开脱，使无罪</td><td>exculpate the accused<br>为被告开脱罪责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exculpated the accused man after ten years in prison.  新证据在这名被告入狱十年后为他开脱了罪责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 absolve</td><td>/əbˈzɑːlv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使无罪，解除责任</td><td>absolve someone of blame<br>免除某人的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report absolved the pilot of all blame.  报告免除了飞行员的全部责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquit</td><td>/əˈkwɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣判无罪</td><td>acquit of murder<br>宣判谋杀罪不成立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jury acquitted him of all charges.  陪审团宣判他所有罪名不成立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exonerate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɑːnəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>免除责备</td><td>exonerate from blame<br>免除责备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exonerated the wrongly jailed man.  新证据为这名被误判入狱者洗清了罪名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vindicate</td><td>/ˈvɪndɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…平反，为…辩护，使无罪；证明，证实</td><td>vindicate one's reputation<br>为自己的名誉洗冤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The results vindicated her unusual approach.  结果证明了她那不寻常的方法是对的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attribute guilt</td><td>/əˈtrɪbjuːt ˈɡɪlt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>归罪于，认定有罪</td><td>attribute guilt to others<br>把罪责归于他人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report attributes guilt to managers who ignored the safety warnings.  报告把罪责归于无视安全警告的管理者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inculpate</del></td><td>/ɪnˈkʌlpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>归罪于</td><td>inculpate an innocent man<br>归罪于无辜者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The forged letter was used to inculpate a loyal officer.  那封伪造的信被用来归罪于一名忠诚的军官。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indict</td><td>/ɪnˈdaɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>起诉，控告</td><td>indict him for bribery<br>起诉他受贿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A grand jury indicted the mayor for taking bribes last year.  大陪审团去年起诉了这位市长受贿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 criminate</del></td><td>/ˈkrɪməneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指控，归罪于</td><td>criminate an accomplice<br>指控一名同伙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His own letters criminated him at the trial.  他自己的信件在审判中给他定了罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incriminate</td><td>/ɪnˈkrɪmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>归罪于</td><td>incriminate oneself<br>使自己受牵连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to answer questions that might incriminate him.  他拒绝回答可能使自己入罪的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>excursive</b></del></td><td>/ɪkˈskɜːrsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>离题的；散漫的</td><td>an excursive narrative<br>散漫的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His excursive lecture wandered far from the announced topic.  他的讲座散漫离题，远离了预告的主题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 desultory</td><td>/ˈdesəlˌtɔːri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>散漫的，无条理的</td><td>desultory conversation<br>散漫的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They made desultory notes instead of a real research plan.  他们做了些散漫的笔记，而非真正的研究计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 digressive</td><td>/daɪˈɡrɛsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>离题的，枝节的</td><td>a digressive lecture<br>离题的讲座</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His digressive style made the short talk feel endless.  他离题的讲法让短短的讲话显得没完没了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meandering</td><td>/miˈændərɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>曲折的；散漫的</td><td>a meandering speech<br>散漫的讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meandering path followed every bend of the small stream.  这条曲折的小径顺着小溪的每个弯道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rambling</td><td>/ˈræmblɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫无目的的，散漫的</td><td>a rambling explanation<br>散漫的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a rambling answer that satisfied nobody present.  他给出散漫的回答，在场无人满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wandering</td><td>/ˈwɑːndərɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>游荡的；散漫的</td><td>a wandering discussion<br>散漫的讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wandering argument never reached any clear conclusion.  这番散漫的论述始终没有明确结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>execrate</b></del></td><td>/ˈeksɪkreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，咒骂</td><td>execrate a tyrant<br>痛斥暴君</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still execrate the tyrant for his cruelty.  史学家仍因其残暴而痛斥这个暴君。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abhor</td><td>/əbˈhɔːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>极度厌恶，深恶痛绝</td><td>abhor violence<br>痛恨暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abhors any form of cruelty to animals.  她极度厌恶任何形式的虐待动物行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abominate</del></td><td>/əˈbɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，厌恶</td><td>abominate cruelty<br>憎恶残忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He abominated the hypocrisy he saw all around him.  他痛恨周围随处可见的虚伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 despise</td><td>/dɪˈspaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鄙视，极其不喜欢</td><td>despise a coward<br>鄙视懦夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She despised him for abandoning his closest friends.  她鄙视他抛弃至交的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detest</td><td>/dɪˈtest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>厌恶，憎恨</td><td>detest hypocrisy<br>厌恶虚伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I detest waiting in long lines on hot days.  我讨厌在炎热的日子里排长队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loathe</td><td>/loʊð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>厌恶，讨厌</td><td>loathe the sight of<br>一见就厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He loathes the smell of boiled cabbage.  他讨厌煮卷心菜的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 love</td><td>/ˈlʌv/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>爱，热爱；喜爱</td><td>fall in love with<br>爱上，与…相爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I love walking along the river in the early morning.  我喜欢清晨沿着河边散步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exemplary</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzempləri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>榜样的，值得效仿的</td><td>exemplary behavior<br>堪为楷模的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young officer received a medal for exemplary conduct under fire.  这名年轻军官因在战火中表现堪为楷模而获得奖章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 archetypal</td><td>/ˌɑːrkɪˈtaɪpəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>典型的，原型的</td><td>an archetypal hero<br>典型的英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Robin Hood is the archetypal outlaw who steals for the poor.  罗宾汉是为穷人偷窃的典型侠盗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 imitable</del></td><td>/ˈɪmətəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可效仿的，值得模仿的</td><td>an imitable style<br>可效仿的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His prose is admirable but hardly imitable by ordinary writers.  他的文章令人钦佩，但普通作者很难效仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 paradigmatic</td><td>/ˌpærədɪɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>典范的，范例性的</td><td>a paradigmatic case<br>典范性的案例</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Economists treat the crash as a paradigmatic case of market panic.  经济学家把这次崩盘视为市场恐慌的典型案例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quintessential</td><td>/ˌkwɪntɪˈsenʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>典型的，精髓的</td><td>the quintessential example<br>最典型的例子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That small cafe is the quintessential Parisian spot for slow mornings.  那家小咖啡馆是巴黎慢晨的典型去处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exempt</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzempt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使免除</td><td>exempt from taxes<br>免税</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new law exempts school textbooks from the sales tax.  新法免除学校教科书的销售税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exhaust</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>耗尽</td><td>exhaust the water supply<br>耗尽水源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two dry summers exhausted the town's entire water supply.  连续两个干燥的夏天耗尽了全镇的水源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consume</td><td>/kənˈsuːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>消耗，耗尽</td><td>consume vast amounts of energy<br>消耗大量能源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Data centers consume vast amounts of electricity every single day.  数据中心每天消耗大量电力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devour</td><td>/dɪˈvaʊər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吞食，耗尽</td><td>devour the entire meal<br>吞掉整顿饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hungry children devoured the entire meal in five minutes.  饥饿的孩子们五分钟内吞掉了整顿饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drain</td><td>/ˈdreɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>耗尽，使枯竭</td><td>drain the country's resources<br>耗尽国家资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long war drained the country's resources and its morale.  漫长的战争耗尽了国家的资源和士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expend</td><td>/ɪkˈspend/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>花费，耗尽</td><td>expend great effort<br>耗费巨大努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They expended most of their savings on repairing the leaking roof.  他们把大部分积蓄花在修补漏雨的屋顶上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spend</td><td>/ˈspend/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>耗尽，用尽；花费</td><td>spend all his strength<br>用尽他全部力气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm had spent its force by the time we arrived.  我们到时风暴已耗尽力量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 use up</td><td>/juːz ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>用完，耗尽</td><td>use up the last supplies<br>用完最后的补给</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We used up the last supplies before reaching the base camp.  我们在到达大本营前用完了最后的补给。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incomplete</td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈpliːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不完全的，未完成的</td><td>an incomplete list<br>不完整的清单</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The survey remains incomplete because two districts never returned their forms.  调查仍不完整，因为两个区从未交回表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 partial</td><td>/ˈpɑːrʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偏袒的，偏爱的；部分的，不完整的</td><td>partial to his friends<br>偏袒他的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee was clearly partial to the home team throughout the match.  整场比赛裁判明显偏袒主队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>exhilarate</b></del></td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪləreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使高兴；使兴奋</td><td>exhilarate the audience<br>使观众兴奋不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fast ride down the mountain exhilarated everyone in the car.  沿山路飞驰而下让车里的每个人都兴奋不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 electrify</td><td>/ɪˈlektrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使激动，使震惊；使电气化</td><td>electrify the audience<br>使观众激动不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her final note electrified the audience and brought them to their feet.  她最后一个音让观众激动得站了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 galvanize</td><td>/ˈɡælvənaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（好似用电击）刺激</td><td>galvanize the team into action<br>激励团队行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach's angry speech galvanized the team into immediate action.  教练愤怒的讲话激励球队立刻行动起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intoxicate</td><td>/ɪnˈtɑːksɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使陶醉；使醉</td><td>intoxicate her with praise<br>用赞美使她陶醉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sudden success seemed to intoxicate the young writer completely.  突来的成功似乎让这位年轻作家彻底陶醉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pump up</td><td>/pʌmp ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使增加，抬高；给…充气</td><td>pump up the price<br>抬高价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Advertising pumped up demand until the small factory could not cope.  广告抬高需求，直到小工厂应付不来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 turn on</td><td>/tɜrn ˈɑn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使兴奋，使感兴趣；打开</td><td>turn on the crowd<br>让人群兴奋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Live jazz really turns him on more than any recorded music.  现场爵士比任何录音都更让他兴奋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sadden</td><td>/ˈsædən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使悲伤</td><td>sadden her deeply<br>使她深感悲伤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of his death saddened the entire small village.  他去世的消息让整个小村庄都感到悲伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depress</td><td>/dɪˈpres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压低，使降低；使沮丧</td><td>depress house prices<br>压低房价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A weak harvest can depress prices for the whole season.  歉收会压低整个季度的价格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soporific</td><td>/ˌsɑːpəˈrɪfɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>催眠的；安眠药</td><td>a mild soporific<br>温和的安眠药</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alcohol acts as a mild soporific, but it ruins deep sleep.  酒精是一种温和的催眠物，但会破坏深度睡眠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exhort</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːrt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>敦促，力劝</td><td>exhort them to persevere<br>力劝他们坚持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speaker exhorted the students to persevere despite repeated failures.  演讲者勉励学生们即使屡遭失败也要坚持下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encourage</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，激励；促进</td><td>encourage students to read<br>鼓励学生阅读</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers encourage students to ask difficult questions.  好老师鼓励学生提出有难度的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 goad</td><td>/ɡoʊd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>刺激，驱使，激发</td><td>goad him into action<br>激他采取行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His teammates goaded him into accepting the ridiculous bet.  队友们激他接受了那个荒唐的赌约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nudge</td><td>/nʌdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>说服某人做某事</td><td>nudge him into agreeing<br>说服他同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her patient questions nudged him into agreeing to see a doctor.  她耐心的询问说服他同意去看医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prod</td><td>/prɑːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促使…行动</td><td>prod her into action<br>促使她行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her friends had to prod her into applying for the scholarship.  朋友们不得不促使她去申请那份奖学金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prompt</td><td>/ˈprɑːmpt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>促使，引起；提示</td><td>prompt an inquiry<br>引发一场调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The leaked memo prompted an immediate inquiry by regulators.  泄露的备忘录促使监管机构立即展开调查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 egg on</td><td>/ɛɡ ˈɑn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>煽动，鼓动</td><td>egg him on<br>鼓动他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His friends egged him on until he agreed to the dare.  朋友们不断鼓动，直到他答应那个挑战。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exigent</b></td><td>/ˈeksɪdʒənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>紧急的；苛求的</td><td>exigent circumstances<br>紧急情况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exigent shortage of water forced the city to ration supplies.  紧急的缺水迫使该市实行配给。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compelling</td><td>/kəmˈpelɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极具说服力的</td><td>a compelling reason<br>令人信服的理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a compelling reason for leaving the project early.  他给出了提前离开项目的极具说服力的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dire</td><td>/ˈdaɪər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的，恐怖的；迫切的</td><td>dire consequences<br>可怕的后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report warned of dire consequences if the dam were not repaired soon.  报告警告说，若不尽快修复大坝，后果将十分可怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emergent</td><td>/ɪˈmɜːrdʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>紧急的，突发的；新兴的</td><td>an emergent crisis<br>突发的危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors must act fast in an emergent situation like this one.  在这样的紧急情况下医生必须迅速行动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperative</td><td>/ɪmˈperətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>命令的，强制性的；迫切的</td><td>an imperative need<br>迫切的需要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is imperative that we repair the dam before the rains arrive.  在雨季来临前修好大坝是迫切必需的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 importunate</td><td>/ɪmˈpɔrtʃənət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迫切的；胡缠的，纠缠不休的</td><td>importunate demands<br>纠缠不休的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The importunate beggar followed us down the whole street.  那个纠缠不休的乞丐跟着我们走了一整条街。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 necessitous</del></td><td>/nəˈsɛsɪtəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；紧急的</td><td>necessitous families<br>贫困的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity was founded to help necessitous widows and orphans.  该慈善机构成立时旨在帮助贫困的寡妇和孤儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pressing</td><td>/ˈpresɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>紧急的，迫切的</td><td>a pressing problem<br>紧迫的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Housing remains the most pressing issue for the new mayor.  住房仍是新市长最紧迫的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 urgent</td><td>/ˈɜːrdʒənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>紧急的，急迫的</td><td>an urgent request<br>紧急的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sent an urgent message asking for immediate help.  她发了一条紧急消息请求立刻援助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 deferrable</del></td><td>/dɪˈfɜrəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可推迟的，不紧急的</td><td>deferrable maintenance<br>可推迟的维护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Treat this repair as deferrable and schedule it for next quarter.  把这项维修视为可推迟的，安排到下季度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 noncritical</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnˈkrɪtɪkəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不关键的，不危急的</td><td>noncritical patients<br>病情不危急的病人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Noncritical systems can be shut down during the upgrade.  非关键系统可以在升级期间关闭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonurgent</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnˈɜrdʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不紧急的</td><td>nonurgent cases<br>非紧急的病例</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nonurgent surgeries were postponed until the following month.  非紧急的手术被推迟到了下个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exodus</b></td><td>/ˈeksədəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大批离去</td><td>a mass exodus<br>大批离去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory closure caused a mass exodus from the small town.  工厂关闭造成大批人离开这个小镇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gush</td><td>/ɡʌʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>喷涌而出；滔滔不绝地说</td><td>gush from a broken pipe<br>从破裂的水管喷涌而出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water gushed from the broken pipe and flooded the basement.  水从破裂的水管喷涌而出，淹了地下室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outpour</td><td>/ˌaʊtˈpɔːr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>倾泻，涌出</td><td>an outpour of grief<br>悲伤的倾泻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letter released an outpour of memories she had long suppressed.  那封信释放出她长期压抑的记忆洪流。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outpouring</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌpɔːrɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>涌出，倾泻；情感迸发</td><td>an outpouring of support<br>支持的涌现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news brought an outpouring of sympathy from readers across the country.  这条消息引来全国读者的同情涌现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 influx</td><td>/ˈɪnflʌks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>涌入</td><td>an influx of tourists<br>游客涌入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden influx of tourists overwhelmed the small town's hotels and restaurants.  游客突然涌入，使这个小镇的旅馆和餐馆不堪重负。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flux</td><td>/ˈflʌks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>流动，涌流；不断变化</td><td>in a state of flux<br>处于不断变化中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole industry is in flux as new regulations take effect.  新规生效后整个行业都在剧烈变动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflow</td><td>/ˈɪnˌfloʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>流入，涌入</td><td>an inflow of capital<br>资本流入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden inflow of foreign money pushed local housing prices higher.  外资突然涌入推高了当地房价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inrush</td><td>/ˈɪnrʌʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>涌入，闯入</td><td>an inrush of cold air<br>冷空气涌入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An inrush of cold air followed him through the heavy door.  一股冷空气随他穿过厚重的门涌入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exonerate</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzɑːnəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>免除责备</td><td>exonerate from blame<br>免除责备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exonerated the wrongly jailed man.  新证据为这名被误判入狱者洗清了罪名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 absolve</td><td>/əbˈzɑːlv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使无罪，解除责任</td><td>absolve someone of blame<br>免除某人的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report absolved the pilot of all blame.  报告免除了飞行员的全部责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquit</td><td>/əˈkwɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣判无罪</td><td>acquit of murder<br>宣判谋杀罪不成立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jury acquitted him of all charges.  陪审团宣判他所有罪名不成立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vindicate</td><td>/ˈvɪndɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…平反，为…辩护，使无罪；证明，证实</td><td>vindicate one's reputation<br>为自己的名誉洗冤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The results vindicated her unusual approach.  结果证明了她那不寻常的方法是对的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 censure</td><td>/ˈsenʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>公开表示反对，谴责</td><td>a vote of censure<br>谴责性投票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to censure the mayor for his conduct.  议会投票谴责市长的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incriminate</td><td>/ɪnˈkrɪmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>归罪于</td><td>incriminate oneself<br>使自己受牵连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to answer questions that might incriminate him.  他拒绝回答可能使自己入罪的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inculpate</del></td><td>/ɪnˈkʌlpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>归罪于</td><td>inculpate an innocent man<br>归罪于无辜者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The forged letter was used to inculpate a loyal officer.  那封伪造的信被用来归罪于一名忠诚的军官。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prove guilty</td><td>/pruːv ˈɡɪlti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>证明有罪</td><td>prove him guilty<br>证明他有罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prosecution failed to prove the defendant guilty beyond reasonable doubt.  检方未能排除合理怀疑地证明被告有罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>exorbitant</b></td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːrbɪtənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的</td><td>exorbitant prices<br>过高的价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourists complained about exorbitant prices for bottled water near the temple.  游客抱怨寺庙附近瓶装水价格过高。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extravagant</td><td>/ɪkˈstrævəɡənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挥霍的；过度的，不必要的</td><td>extravagant spending<br>挥霍的开支</td></tr>
