<tr><td colspan="7">例 He feigned surprise when they announced the award.  他们宣布获奖时他装出惊讶的样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invent</td><td>/ˌɪnˈvent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>发明；编造</td><td>invent an excuse<br>编造借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He invented a clumsy excuse for missing the morning meeting.  他为缺席早会编了个拙劣的借口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>facetious</b></td><td>/fəˈsiːʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喜欢开玩笑的，轻浮的</td><td>a facetious remark<br>轻浮的玩笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His facetious remark at the funeral offended nearly everyone present.  他在葬礼上轻浮的玩笑话得罪了几乎所有在场的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 humorous</td><td>/ˈhjuːmərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>幽默的，滑稽的</td><td>a humorous remark<br>幽默的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her humorous account of the trip had everyone laughing.  她对旅行的幽默叙述让大家都笑了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 jocose</del></td><td>/dʒoʊˈkoʊs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诙谐的，爱开玩笑的</td><td>a jocose tone<br>诙谐的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His jocose manner put the nervous guests at ease.  他诙谐的举止让紧张的客人放松下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jocular</td><td>/ˈdʒɑːkjələr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>搞笑的，欢乐的</td><td>a jocular remark<br>诙谐的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His jocular tone kept the tense meeting from turning truly hostile.  他诙谐的语气让紧张的会议没有真正翻脸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 waggish</del></td><td>/ˈwæɡɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>滑稽的，爱开玩笑的</td><td>a waggish grin<br>顽皮的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waggish waiter kept teasing us about our accents.  这个爱开玩笑的服务员一直拿我们的口音逗趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 witty</td><td>/ˈwɪti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>机智诙谐的</td><td>a witty reply<br>机智的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her witty toast at the wedding delighted every guest.  她在婚礼上机智的祝酒词让每位客人都很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 earnest</td><td>/ˈɜːrnɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃认真的</td><td>an earnest student<br>认真的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An earnest student stayed behind to ask about the summer reading list.  一名认真的学生留下来询问暑期阅读书目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sincere</td><td>/sɪnˈsɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真诚的，诚挚的</td><td>a sincere apology<br>真诚的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sincere apology repaired a friendship damaged for years.  她真诚的道歉修复了受损多年的友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>facile</b></td><td>/ˈfæsl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易做到的；肤浅轻率的</td><td>a facile explanation<br>肤浅的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report offers a facile explanation for a very complex problem.  这份报告对一个非常复杂的问题给出了肤浅的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cursory</td><td>/ˈkɜːrsəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，不注意细节的</td><td>a cursory glance<br>匆匆一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cursory inspection missed the cracks running along the bridge supports.  一次匆忙的检查漏掉了桥墩上的裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shallow</td><td>/ˈʃæloʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浅薄的，肤浅的；浅的</td><td>shallow water<br>浅水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His arguments are shallow and easy to refute.  他的论点浅薄，很容易反驳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 simplistic</td><td>/sɪmˈplɪstɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过分简单化的</td><td>a simplistic explanation<br>过分简单的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That simplistic view ignores decades of careful research.  这种过分简单的看法忽视了数十年的细致研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superficial</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈfɪʃl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面的，肤浅的</td><td>a superficial understanding<br>肤浅的理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has only a superficial knowledge of the subject.  她对这门学科只有肤浅的了解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deep</td><td>/ˈdiːp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深刻的，深奥的</td><td>a deep analysis<br>深刻的分析</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The essay offers a deep reading of the novel's ending.  这篇文章对小说结尾作了深刻解读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 profound</td><td>/proʊˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深刻的，深远的</td><td>a profound influence<br>深远的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her death had a profound effect on the whole family.  她的死对整个家庭产生了深远的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>facilitate</b></td><td>/fəˈsɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变容易，促进</td><td>facilitate the discussion<br>促进讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new airport will facilitate tourism throughout the whole region.  新机场将促进整个地区的旅游业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expedite</td><td>/ˈekspədaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加快进程</td><td>expedite the process<br>加快流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hiring two more clerks expedited the review of every application.  又雇了两名职员加快了每份申请的审核。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forward</td><td>/ˈfɔːrwərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>向前的；前部的</td><td>take a forward step<br>向前迈一步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He leaned forward to hear the quiet speaker better.  他向前倾身以便听清声音轻的讲话者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 further</td><td>/ˈfɜːrðər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>促进，推动；更远的</td><td>further his career<br>促进他的事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took the job abroad to further her research on glaciers.  她接受海外工作以推进她的冰川研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 help</td><td>/ˈhelp/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>帮助，助益</td><td>a great help<br>很大的帮助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your advice was a great help during the difficult week.  在那困难的一周里你的建议帮了大忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 check</td><td>/tʃek/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使突然停止，阻止；检查，核对</td><td>check the spread of disease<br>阻止疾病蔓延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict quarantine measures checked the spread of the disease.  严格的隔离措施阻止了疾病的蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hamper</td><td>/ˈhæmpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍；阻碍；束缚</td><td>hamper the rescue effort<br>妨碍救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow hampered the rescue teams trying to reach the stranded climbers.  大雪妨碍了试图接近被困登山者的救援队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 handicap</td><td>/ˈhændikæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>妨碍，使不利；障碍</td><td>handicap the team<br>使球队处于不利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor equipment handicapped the researchers from the very start.  设备不足从一开始就妨碍了研究人员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hinder</td><td>/ˈhɪndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>hinder progress<br>妨碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor lighting hindered the search for survivors.  照明不足妨碍了对生还者的搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impede</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻碍</td><td>impede progress<br>阻碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog impeded the rescue team's progress.  浓雾阻碍了救援队的前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstruct</td><td>/əbˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻塞</td><td>obstruct justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fallen tree obstructed the only road out of town.  一棵倒下的树堵住了唯一的出城道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thwart</td><td>/θwɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻挠，挫败</td><td>thwart an attempt<br>挫败一次尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alert guards thwarted the escape attempt.  警觉的守卫挫败了这次越狱企图。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 retard</td><td>/rɪˈtɑːrd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减速，延迟</td><td>retard the growth<br>延缓生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Language barriers retarded the progress of the peace negotiations.  语言障碍阻碍了和平谈判的进展。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>faction</b></td><td>/ˈfækʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>派系</td><td>a rival faction<br>敌对派系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A rival faction inside the party blocked the reform bill.  党内一个敌对派系阻挠了改革法案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bloc</td><td>/ˈblɑːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>集团，联盟</td><td>a voting bloc<br>投票集团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farming bloc firmly opposed the new tariff bill.  农业集团坚决反对新的关税法案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 body</td><td>/ˈbɑːdi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>体现，具体表现；身体</td><td>body forth an idea<br>体现某种思想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statue seems to body forth every ideal the founders cherished.  这座雕像仿佛体现了创建者珍视的每一个理想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clique</td><td>/ˈkliːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>派系，小团体</td><td>a tight clique<br>紧密的小团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small clique controlled every decision in the department.  一个小团体控制着系里的每项决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coalition</td><td>/ˌkoʊəˈlɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>联合，联盟</td><td>form a coalition<br>组成联盟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three parties formed a coalition to pass the budget.  三个党派组成联盟以通过预算。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sect</td><td>/ˈsekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>教派，派别</td><td>a religious sect<br>一个宗教派别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sect broke away from the main church long ago.  该教派很久以前就从主教会分离出去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wing</td><td>/ˈwɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>派系，翼；翅膀</td><td>the radical wing<br>激进派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reformist wing of the party demanded an open vote.  党内改革派要求公开投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fallacious</b></td><td>/fəˈleɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谬误的，不合逻辑的</td><td>a fallacious argument<br>谬误的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer exposed the fallacious reasoning behind the entire claim.  律师揭穿了整个主张背后的谬误推理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illogical</td><td>/ˌɪˈlɑːdʒɪkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合逻辑的，不合理的</td><td>an illogical conclusion<br>不合逻辑的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems illogical to cut staff while demand keeps rising.  在需求持续上升时裁员似乎不合逻辑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invalid</td><td>/ɪnˈvælɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无效的；站不住脚的</td><td>an invalid argument<br>站不住脚的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract is invalid because it was never signed.  这份合同无效，因为从未签署。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irrational</td><td>/ˌɪˈræʃənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不理性的，无理性的</td><td>an irrational fear<br>非理性的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has an irrational fear of elevators and always takes stairs.  他对电梯有非理性的恐惧，总是走楼梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mad</td><td>/ˈmæd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>疯狂的，荒唐的；愤怒的</td><td>a mad scheme<br>荒唐的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It would be mad to sell the house at that price.  以那个价格卖房子简直荒唐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 reasonless</del></td><td>/ˈriːzənləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无理由的，不合理的</td><td>reasonless fear<br>无缘由的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His reasonless hostility puzzled everyone at the meeting.  他毫无理由的敌意让会上所有人困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 sophistic</del></td><td>/səˈfɪstɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诡辩的，似是而非的</td><td>sophistic reasoning<br>诡辩式的推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer's sophistic argument collapsed under close questioning.  律师的诡辩论证在细致质询下崩塌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unreasonable</td><td>/ənˈriːznəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合理的，过分的</td><td>unreasonable demands<br>不合理的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Asking staff to work three weekends running is unreasonable.  要求员工连续三个周末加班是不合理的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sound</td><td>/saʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>牢固的；健康强壮的；（逻辑）严谨的</td><td>sound financial advice<br>稳妥的理财建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyer gave sound financial advice that saved the family thousands.  她的律师给出稳妥的理财建议，为家里省下数千元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fallow</b></td><td>/ˈfæloʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>休耕的</td><td>leave the field fallow<br>让田地休耕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer left the tired field fallow for a whole season.  农民让这块贫瘠的田地整季休耕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 untilled</del></td><td>/ʌnˈtɪld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未耕作的，荒着的</td><td>untilled land<br>未耕作的土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The untilled fields were slowly taken over by weeds.  未耕作的田地慢慢被杂草占据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncultivated</td><td>/ʌnˈkʌltɪveɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未开垦的；未受教养的</td><td>uncultivated soil<br>未开垦的土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Much of the island remains uncultivated and covered in scrub.  岛上大部分地方仍未开垦，长满灌木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>failsafe</b></td><td>/ˈfeɪlseɪf/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>故障防护的；万无一失的</td><td>a failsafe mechanism<br>故障防护装置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reactor has a failsafe system that shuts it down automatically.  这座反应堆有一套自动停机的故障防护系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caution</td><td>/ˈkɔːʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>谨慎；警告，告诫</td><td>proceed with caution<br>谨慎行事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guide urged caution on the icy mountain path.  向导叮嘱在结冰的山路上要谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 palladium</td><td>/pəˈleɪdiəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>保障，保护物</td><td>a palladium of liberty<br>自由的保障</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A free press is often called the palladium of democracy.  自由的新闻界常被称为民主的保障。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preventive</td><td>/prɪˈventɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>预防的；预防措施</td><td>preventive measures<br>预防措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular inspections are the cheapest preventive measure available.  定期检查是现有最便宜的预防措施。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 safeguard</td><td>/ˈseɪfɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>保护措施</td><td>a legal safeguard<br>法律保障</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rules act as a safeguard against careless lending.  新规定是对草率放贷的一道保障。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 certain</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，无疑的</td><td>certain of the outcome<br>对结果有把握</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are certain that the letter was never sent.  我们确信那封信从未寄出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foolproof</td><td>/ˈfuːlpruːf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>十分简单以至于不会失败的</td><td>a foolproof plan<br>万无一失的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineers promised a foolproof plan, but the machine jammed anyway.  工程师承诺了万无一失的方案，机器却还是卡住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sure</td><td>/ˈʃʊr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，可靠的</td><td>a sure sign<br>可靠的迹象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Falling leaves are a sure sign that autumn has arrived.  落叶是秋天已到的可靠迹象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfailing</td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无穷的，可靠的</td><td>unfailing patience<br>无穷的耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed unfailing patience with the youngest students.  她对最小的学生表现出无穷的耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fallible</td><td>/ˈfæləbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可能出错的</td><td>a fallible memory<br>可能出错的记忆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even the best experts are fallible, so check their figures.  即便最优秀的专家也可能出错，所以要核对他们的数字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
